Saudi Arabia: 5 arrested for cross-dressing on social media DUBAI 31°C GOLD/FOREX PRAYER TIMES NEWSLETTERS EPAPER GOLD/FOREX DUBAI 31°C PRAYER TIMES GULF GULF BAHRAIN KUWAIT OMAN QATAR SAUDI YEMEN World / Gulf / Saudi Saudi Arabia: 5 arrested for cross-dressing on social media Men dressed in women’s clothes filmed footage, posted it on social media Last updated: October 25, 2021 | 03:51 Samir Salama, Associate Editor 1 MIN READ Add as a preferred source on Google Shutterstock Shutterstock Also In This Package Pictures: Grand mosque in Mecca opens at full capacity In pictures: Sheikh Mohammed visits Expo 2020 Dubai World reopens: Saudis allow 100% capacity A look inside the record-holding Saudi Arabia Pavilion Abu Dhabi: Five cross-dressing Saudi men were arrested for daring to walk the streets of the Northern Borders province, bordering Iraq and Jordan, taking a video and posting it on social media, local media reported. “What the men did was inconsistent with the public morals of society,” a spokesperson for the Northern Borders Police said. The Saudi government prohibits cross-dressing and being transgender. Criminal sanctions for cross-dressing tend to be the same for homosexuality: imprisonment, fines and, for foreigners, deportation.
